(1a) * Punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever for remuneration or regularly provides health services by unlawfully presenting themselves as a psychotherapist.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if quackery is committed by simulating authorization to practice medicine.
(3) For the purposes of this Section, a person authorized to practice medicine is one who,
a) has a general medical or dental degree obtained at a domestic university,
b) has a general medical or dental degree obtained at a foreign university and naturalized, provided with an equivalency clause,
c) is a foreign national who may carry out medical activity on the basis of a permit without naturalization of their degree,
provided that they are not subject to a prohibition from medical activity.
(4) Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of quackery.
18

## 8. §

(1) Whoever unlawfully manufactures, possesses, uses or places poison on the market, or whoever fails to take measures prescribed to prevent the abusive use of poisons or to exclude the endangerment of other persons, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.
(2) For the purposes of this Section, poison means a hazardous substance or mixture which, based on its toxicological properties, in case of inhalation, ingestion or absorption through the skin, endangers human life or may cause serious injury or serious harm to health.
18

## 9. §

(1) Whoever manufactures or possesses for the purpose of placing on the market a consumer article that is harmful to health shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) Whoever places a harmful consumer article on the market shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the criminal offense is committed
a) with respect to a significant quantity or value of harmful consumer articles,
b) in a criminal association, or
c) as a business
(4) Whoever commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(5) The perpetrator of the criminal offense defined in paragraphs (1) and (4) shall not be punishable if, as soon as they become aware of the harmful nature of the consumer article, they do everything to ensure that the harmful consumer article is returned to their possession or is not placed on the market.
19

## 0. § (1) Whoever deprives another of their personal liberty

a) by violence or by direct threat against life or bodily integrity, or
b) by placing them in a state incapable of defense or expression of will or by exploiting such a state
and makes their release dependent on the fulfillment of a demand, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if the kidnapping is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) in a criminal association,
c) while armed,
d) in an armed state, or
e) against an official person or foreign official person, with regard to this capacity.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen,
b) causing particularly serious disadvantage, or
c) causing death.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from ten years to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping also constitutes intentional homicide.
(5) Whoever commits preparation for kidnapping shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(6) The punishment of a person who voluntarily abandons the kidnapping – before serious consequences have resulted from it – may be reduced without limitation.
19

## 1. §

Whoever obtains credible knowledge that the commission of a kidnapping is being prepared and does not inform the affected person or the authority as soon as possible, if the kidnapping is attempted or committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
19

## 2. § * (1) Whoever

a) sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives another as consideration, or
b) transports, accommodates, conceals, or obtains for another for the purpose of committing the act defined in point a),
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) Whoever for the purpose of obtaining regular advantage induces another to perform work, work-like activity, other service, or to engage in unlawful conduct
a) by deception, by abusing a power or influence relationship related to the victim's upbringing, supervision, care, medical treatment, or other power or influence relationship with the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation, or
b) by coercion with violence or threat,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.
(3) Whoever for the purpose of committing the act defined in paragraph (2) recruits, transfers, receives, sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives as consideration, obtains for another, transports, accommodates, conceals another, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.
(4) Whoever commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) for the purpose of performing a sexual act or unlawful use of the human body shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.
(5) The punishment in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five years to ten years, in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if it is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) with the maltreatment of the victim,
c) as an official person, using this capacity, or
d) against multiple persons.
(6) The punishment in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five years to twenty years or life imprisonment, if it is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve, or
b) causing danger to life or particularly serious disadvantage.
(7) Whoever commits preparation for human trafficking and forced labor, in the case defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, in the case defined in paragraph (2) or (3) for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years, in the case defined in paragraph (4) by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(8) Whoever
a) uses or utilizes the work, work-like activity, other service or unlawful conduct of the victim of human trafficking and forced labor defined in paragraph (2), shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years,
b) commits the criminal offense defined in point a) by using a sexual act or for the purpose of unlawful use of the human body, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.
19

## 3. § *

19

## 4. §

(1) Whoever deprives another of their personal liberty shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the violation of personal liberty is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) from a base motive or for a base purpose,
c) with the maltreatment of the victim,
d) against a person incapable of defense,
e) while armed,
f) in an armed state,
g) causing significant harm to interests, or
h) by simulating official proceedings.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the criminal offense committed against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen also qualifies under point b) or c) of paragraph (2).
19

## 5. §

Anyone who compels another person by force or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, and thereby causes significant harm to their interests, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years, if no other criminal offence is committed.
19

## 6. §

(1) Anyone who compels another person to perform or tolerate a sexual act shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) Anyone who commits sexual coercion
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) against their relative, or a person under their upbringing, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim
shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the sexual coercion is committed against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen.
19

## 7. §

(1) Anyone who commits sexual violence and shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years, who
a) commits sexual coercion by force or by direct threat against life or physical integrity,
b) exploits another person's state of being incapable of defence or expression of will for a sexual act.
(2) Anyone who performs or causes to be performed a sexual act with a person who has not reached the age of twelve also commits sexual violence and shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) by the perpetrator against their relative, or a person under their upbringing, supervision, care, medical treatment, or otherwise under their power or influence, or
c) by several persons on the same occasion, with knowledge of each other's acts.
(4) * Anyone shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to twenty years,
a) who commits the criminal offence defined in paragraph (2)
aa) in the manner defined in paragraph (1),
ab) against the victim defined in paragraph (3) point b), or
ac) in the manner defined in paragraph (3) point c),
or
b) if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (3) point a) also qualifies under paragraph (3) point b) or c).
(4a) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (4) point a) subpoint aa) also qualifies under paragraph (4) point a) subpoint ab) or ac).
(5) Anyone who provides conditions necessary for or facilitating the commission of sexual violence shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
19

## 8. §

(1) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who performs a sexual act with a person who has not reached the age of fourteen, or induces such person to perform a sexual act with another, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who seeks to induce a person who has not reached the age of fourteen to perform a sexual act with them or with another shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) If the victim is a relative of the perpetrator or is under their upbringing, supervision, care or medical treatment, or the perpetrator commits the sexual abuse by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in paragraph (1), imprisonment from two to eight years,
b) in the case defined in paragraph (2), imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who performs a sexual act with a person who has reached the age of fourteen but has not reached the age of eighteen by abusing a power or influence relationship existing in relation to them shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
19

## 9. §

(1) Anyone who performs a sexual act with their lineal relative shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) Anyone who has sexual intercourse with their sibling shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(3) A descendant shall not be punishable for the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) if they had not reached the age of eighteen at the time of committing the act.
20

## 0. §

(1) Anyone who procures someone for another person to perform a sexual act for the purpose of gain shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) * Anyone who offers or solicits a person who has not reached the age of eighteen for prostitution commits procuring and shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(3) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the procuring is committed as a business.
(3a) * If the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)–(3) is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,
b) in the case defined in paragraph (2), imprisonment from two to eight years,
c) in the case defined in paragraph (3), imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the procuring defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) by the perpetrator against their relative, a person under their upbringing, supervision or care, or by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim,
c) by deception, force or threat.
(5) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if
a) the procuring defined in paragraph (4) point a) or b) also qualifies under point c),
b) the procuring defined in any of the points of paragraph (4) is committed as a special recidivist.
(6) Anyone who agrees to commit procuring as a business shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
20

## 1. § (1) Anyone who

a) induces another person to engage in prostitution,
b) makes a building or other place available to another for the purpose of prostitution,
c) *
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) * Anyone who commits the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) in connection with the prostitution of a person who has not reached the age of eighteen, or provides assistance for the prostitution of a person who has not reached the age of eighteen, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) Anyone who maintains, manages a brothel, or provides material resources for its operation shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) * If the facilitation of prostitution is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,
b) in the case defined in paragraph (2), imprisonment from five to ten years,
c) in the case defined in paragraph (3), imprisonment from two to eight years.
20

## 2. §

Anyone who has themselves wholly or partly supported by a person engaged in prostitution shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
20

## 3. §

(1) * Anyone who seeks to gain profit from the prostitution of a person who has not reached the age of eighteen shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) * Anyone who provides consideration for a sexual act with a person who has not reached the age of eighteen shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) * Anyone who has themselves partly or wholly supported by a person engaged in prostitution who has not reached the age of eighteen shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) Anyone who maintains, manages a brothel, or provides material resources for the operation of a brothel in which a person who has not reached the age of eighteen engages in prostitution shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(5) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the exploitation of child prostitution is committed as a special recidivist.
20

## 4. §

* (1) Whoever, in relation to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has not attained the age of eighteen,
a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) offers, transfers or makes it accessible, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years,
c) produces, places on the market, trades in it, or makes such recording accessible to the general public, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five years to ten years in the case of point b), and from five years to fifteen years in the case of point c), if the offence defined therein is committed
a) against a person who has not attained the age of twelve,
b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship in relation to the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation,
c) as an official person by using this capacity,
d) in relation to a recording containing maltreatment or the use of violence, or
e) as a special recidivist.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five years to fifteen years in the case of point b), and from five years to twenty years in the case of point c), if the offence defined therein is committed in relation to a recording containing maltreatment or the use of violence against a person who has not attained the age of twelve.
(4) Whoever
a) provides material resources for the offence defined in point c) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) commits preparatory acts for the offence defined in point c) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) Whoever, in relation to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has attained the age of fourteen but has not attained the age of eighteen,
a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years,
b) produces it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exists.
(6) Whoever invites a person or persons who has/have not attained the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(7) Whoever invites a person or persons who has/have attained the age of fourteen but has/have not attained the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exists.
(8) For the purposes of this §, pornographic recording means: the depiction of another person or other persons with sexuality in a manner that is seriously offensive to decency, with openness, purposefully aimed at arousing sexual desire, including the realistic depiction of a non-existent person or persons.
204/A. § * (1) Whoever
a) participates in a pornographic performance in which a person or persons who has/have not attained the age of eighteen appears/appear, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years,
b) features a person or persons who has/have not attained the age of eighteen in a pornographic performance or organises such a pornographic performance, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five years to fifteen years in the case of point b), if the offence defined therein is committed
a) against a person who has not attained the age of twelve,
b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship in relation to the victim,
c) as an official person by using this capacity,
d) in relation to a performance presenting maltreatment or the use of violence, or
e) as a special recidivist.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to fifteen years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five years to twenty years in the case of point b), if the offence defined therein is committed in relation to a performance presenting maltreatment or the use of violence against a person who has not attained the age of twelve.
(4) Whoever
a) provides material resources for the offence defined in point b) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) commits preparatory acts for the offence defined in point b) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) Whoever invites a person or persons who has/have not attained the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(6) Whoever invites a person or persons who has/have attained the age of fourteen but has/have not attained the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exists.
(7) For the purposes of this §, pornographic performance means: an act or presentation displaying the sexuality of another person or other persons with seriously offensive openness to decency, purposefully aimed at arousing sexual desire.
20

## 5. §

(1) Whoever displays himself/herself in a manner offensive to decency before another person for the purpose of arousing or satisfying his/her sexual desire, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to two years.
(2) A person who has attained the age of eighteen who engages in conduct offensive to decency before a person who has not attained the age of fourteen for the purpose of arousing or satisfying his/her sexual desire, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(3) If a more serious offence is not committed, whoever engages in conduct offensive to decency towards another person which violates the victim's human dignity, shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).
20

## 6. §

Expulsion may also be imposed against the perpetrator of exploitation of child prostitution, facilitation of prostitution, being kept and indecent exposure.
20

## 7. §

The offence defined in paragraph (1) of 19

## 6. §

, in point a) of paragraph (1) of 19

## 7. §

, and in paragraph (3) of 20

## 5. §

is punishable only upon private motion, except where an offence not punishable upon private motion is also committed in connection therewith.
207/A. § * For the purposes of paragraph (3a) of 20

## 0. §

, point b) of paragraph (5) of 20

## 0. §

, paragraph (4) of 20

## 1. §

and paragraph (5) of 20

## 3. §

, an offence of a similar nature for the purposes of special recidivism is trafficking in human beings and forced labour, procuring, facilitation of prostitution and exploitation of child prostitution.
20

## 8. §

(1) A person obliged to educate, supervise or care for a minor – including the cohabiting partner of a parent or guardian exercising parental supervision, as well as a parent deprived of parental supervision rights if living in a common household or dwelling with the minor – who seriously violates his/her duty arising from this task and thereby endangers the minor's physical, intellectual, moral or emotional development, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) If a more serious offence is not committed, a person who has attained the age of eighteen who
a) induces or attempts to induce a person who has not attained the age of eighteen to commit an offence or a petty offence, or to pursue a depraved lifestyle,
b) offers a person who has not attained the age of eighteen for the commission of an offence,
shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).
20

## 9. § Whoever

a) violates the statutory provisions on the employment of persons who have not attained the age of eighteen, or
b) * employs a third-country national who has not attained the age of eighteen and does not hold a permit issued for the purpose of work,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
21

## 0. §

(1) A person who, through their own fault, obstructs the establishment or maintenance of contact between a minor placed with them by an official decision and a person entitled to contact with the minor, even after the imposition of a fine applied to enforce contact, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) * The offender shall not be punishable if they adequately ensure contact until the first-instance decision on the merits is made and begins to make up for the missed forms of contact.
21

## 1. §

(1) A person who takes away a minor placed by an enforceable official decision from the person with whom the authority placed them, without that person's consent, for the purpose of permanently changing the placement, or keeps the minor hidden or in secret, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) A person who, in committing the offense specified in paragraph (1), uses violence at the time of taking away or direct threat against life or bodily integrity, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
21

## 2. §

(1) A person who, through their own fault, fails to fulfill their child support obligation prescribed by law and in an enforceable official decision, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) A person who, through their own fault, fails to fulfill their maintenance obligation prescribed by law and in an enforceable official decision, and thereby subjects the beneficiary to severe deprivation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) * The offender shall not be punishable under paragraph (1), and in the case of paragraph (2) their punishment may be reduced without limitation, if they fulfill their obligation until the first-instance decision on the merits is made.
212/A. § * (1) A person who, against their child's parent, and furthermore against a relative, former spouse, former cohabiting partner, guardian, ward, or person under guardianship who at the time of the offense or previously lived in a common household or dwelling with them, regularly
a) engages in conduct that seriously violates human dignity, is humiliating and violent,
b) withholds material assets belonging to the common economy or common property and thereby subjects the victim to severe deprivation,
if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) A person who regularly commits against the person specified in paragraph (1)
a) bodily harm under Section 16

## 4.

paragraph (2) or defamation under Section 22

## 7. § paragraph (2), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years,

b) bodily harm under Section 16

## 4.

paragraphs (3) and (4), violation of personal liberty or coercion under Section 19

## 4.

paragraph (1), by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(3) Expulsion may also be imposed against a perpetrator of domestic violence.
(4) The offense specified in paragraph (1) shall be punishable only upon private motion.
21

## 3. §

(1) A person who changes or terminates another person's family status shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the violation of family status is committed
a) by an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution in the course of their occupation,
b) by a person responsible for the upbringing, supervision or care of a person who has not reached the age of eighteen.
(3) If the violation of family status is committed by an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution in the course of their occupation through negligence, they shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
21

## 4. §

A person who enters into a new marriage during the existence of their marriage, or who enters into marriage with a married person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
21

## 5. § A person who

a) restricts another person in their freedom of conscience by violence or threat,
b) obstructs another person in the free exercise of their religion by violence or threat,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
21

## 6. §

(1) A person who, because of another person's membership or perceived membership in a national, ethnic, racial, religious group or certain groups of the population, including in particular their disability, gender identity, sexual orientation, engages in provocatively anti-social conduct that is capable of causing alarm in a member of that group, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) A person who mistreats another person, or coerces them by violence or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, because of their membership or perceived membership in a national, ethnic, racial, religious group or certain groups of the population, including in particular their disability, gender identity, sexual orientation, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if violence against a member of a community is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) causing significant harm to interests,
d) with the maltreatment of the victim,
e) in a group, or
f) in a criminal association.
(4) A person who commits preparation for violence against a member of a community shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
21

## 7. §

* (1) A person who obstructs another person in the exercise of their right to association or assembly by violence or threat shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) A person who obstructs, makes impossible, or otherwise frustrates the conduct of an assembly, or seriously disrupts the assembly, if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
217/A. § * A person who resists the organizer, leader or stewards of an assembly by violence or threat during the lawful performance of their stewarding duties at the assembly, or mistreats them while they are performing their stewarding duties, if another offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
217/B. § * (1) A person who violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful nature of an assembly under the law on the right of assembly, if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The leader of an assembly shall be punishable under paragraph (1) if a steward employed by them violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful nature of the assembly and they do not take immediate action to remove the steward upon becoming aware of it.
217/C. § * A person who organizes a prohibited assembly or calls for participation in it under the law on the right of assembly, if another offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
21

## 8. § (1) A person who performs

a) a medical intervention requiring consent or authorization related to alteration of the human genome, alteration of the genome of an embryo, human reproduction or selection of the sex of a child to be born,
b) medical research that may be performed on a human, embryo or gamete,
c) organ or tissue removal or organ or tissue transplantation performed for the purpose of transplantation
without the consent or authorization of the person entitled, or fails to provide the appropriate information required by law for the exercise of the right to consent and authorization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) A person who removes an organ or tissue from a deceased person contrary to a declaration of objection shall be punishable under paragraph (1).
(3) A person who, through negligence, performs a medical intervention, medical research, organ or tissue removal, or organ or tissue transplantation requiring consent or authorization under paragraph (1) without the consent or authorization of the person entitled, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
21

## 9. §

(1) * Anyone who, in violation of the provisions laid down in legislation on the protection or processing of personal data or in a binding legal act of the European Union, for the purpose of gaining profit or causing significant harm to interests,
a) processes personal data unlawfully or for a purpose other than intended, or
b) fails to take measures to ensure the security of data,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) * Anyone who, in violation of the provisions laid down in legislation on the protection or processing of personal data or in a binding legal act of the European Union, fails to fulfil their obligation to provide information necessary for the data subject to exercise their right of access, and thereby significantly harms the interests of another person or persons, shall also be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).
(3) * The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the misuse of personal data is committed in relation to special data or criminal personal data.
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the misuse of personal data is committed as an official person or by using a public mandate.
22

## 0. §

(1) Anyone who, in violation of the statutory provisions on the publicity of data of public interest,
a) conceals data of public interest from the data requester, or fails to fulfil their obligation to provide information after the court has finally ordered the disclosure of the data of public interest,
b) makes data of public interest inaccessible or falsifies it, or
c) makes false or falsified data of public interest accessible or public,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the misuse of data of public interest is committed for the purpose of unlawful gain.
22

## 1. §

(1) Anyone who enters another person's dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto by force, threat or by simulating official proceedings, or remains there, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) Anyone who enters another person's dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto, against the will of the person residing there or having control over it, or by deception,
a) at night,
b) armed,
c) equipped with weapons, or
d) in a group,
or remains there, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) Anyone who prevents another person from entering their dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto,
a) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (1), shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1),
b) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2), shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (2).
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony if the offence specified in paragraph (1) is committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2).
22

## 2. §

(1) Anyone who, for the purpose of intimidating another person or arbitrarily interfering with another person's private life or daily life, harasses them regularly or persistently, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) Anyone who, for the purpose of causing fear,
a) threatens another person or their relative with the commission of a punishable act involving violence against a person or causing public danger, or
b) creates the appearance that an event will occur that harms or directly endangers another person's life, physical integrity or health,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(3) * Anyone who commits harassment
a) against their spouse, former spouse, cohabitant or former cohabitant,
b) against a person under their upbringing, supervision, care or medical treatment,
c) by abusing their position of power or influence, or
d) against an official person, in a place or at a time incompatible with their official activity,
shall be punishable in the case specified in paragraph (1) by imprisonment not exceeding two years, and in the case specified in paragraph (2) by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(4) * A person who has reached the age of eighteen and commits the harassment specified in paragraph (1) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
22

## 3. §

(1) Anyone who, without good cause, discloses a private secret that came to their knowledge by virtue of their occupation or public mandate shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by confinement.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offence causes significant harm to interests.
22

## 4. § (1) Anyone who

a) destroys another person's closed consignment containing a communication, opens it for the purpose of learning its content, obtains it, or hands it over to an unauthorised person for such purpose, or
b) * intercepts a communication transmitted to another person via an electronic communications network – including an information system – if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by confinement.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offence specified in paragraph (1) is committed by using an occupation or public mandate.
(3) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offence specified in paragraph (1),
b) imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offence specified in paragraph (2)
causes significant harm to interests.
22

## 5. §

(1) Anyone who, by exploiting another person's vulnerable life situation, induces them to engage in self-degrading behaviour, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the perpetrator
a) gives or promises consideration during the inducement to engage in the behaviour specified in paragraph (1),
b) makes a recording of the self-degrading person or makes such a recording accessible to the general public.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offence is committed as a business.
22

## 6. §

(1) Anyone who asserts or spreads a fact suitable for impairing the honour of someone in the presence of another person, or uses an expression directly referring to such a fact, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the defamation is committed
a) from or for a base motive or purpose,
b) before a large public, or
c) causing significant harm to interests.
(3) * The act of a person who commits the acts specified in paragraph (1) through a press product or media service in the context of the free discussion of public affairs shall not be punishable for defamation, provided that their act is not directed at the manifestly and seriously defamatory denial of the victim's human dignity.
226/A. § * (1) Anyone who, for the purpose of impairing the honour of another person or persons, makes a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording, if another criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
226/B. § * (1) Anyone who, for the purpose of impairing the honour of another person or persons, makes a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording accessible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) * The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offence is committed
a) before a large public, or
b) causing significant harm to interests.
22

## 7. §

(1) Anyone who, in addition to the acts specified in § 22

## 6., against another person

a) in connection with the victim's performance of their job, fulfilment of their public mandate or public interest activity, or
b) before a large public
uses an expression suitable for impairing honour or commits another such act, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) Anyone who commits the insult by assault shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).
(3) * The act of a person who commits the acts specified in paragraph (1) through a press product or media service in the context of the free discussion of public affairs shall not be punishable for insult, provided that their act is not directed at the manifestly and seriously defamatory denial of the victim's human dignity.
22

## 8. §

Whoever desecrates a deceased person or their memory in the manner specified in Section 226 or 22

## 7. §

shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with the penalty specified therein.
22

## 9. §

(1) The perpetrator shall not be punishable for the offense specified in Sections 226–22

## 8. §

if the fact capable of impairing honor proves to be true.
(2) Proof of truth is admissible if the assertion or spreading of the fact, or the use of an expression directly referring to it, was justified by the public interest or the legitimate interest of anyone.
23

## 0. § Against the perpetrator of harassment, restraining order may also be imposed

23

## 1. §

(1) The offense specified in Section 21

## 8. §

shall be punishable only upon private motion, except if a criminal offense punishable otherwise than upon private motion is also committed in connection with it. In the case of Section 21

## 8. §

paragraph (2), the private motion may be submitted by the person entitled to make a declaration as specified in the Act on Healthcare, or by a relative or heir of the deceased.
(2) The offense specified in Sections 221–22

## 8. §

shall be punishable only upon private motion, except if the offense specified in Section 22

## 7. §

is committed against a member of a law enforcement agency.
(3) In the case of Section 22

## 8. §

the private motion may be submitted by a relative or heir of the deceased.
23

## 2. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another person or other persons by damaging or destroying a traffic route, vehicle, industrial equipment or their accessories, by creating an obstacle, by removing or altering traffic signals, by deceptive signaling, by using violence or threat against the driver of a moving vehicle, or in another similar manner, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for the offense specified in paragraph (1) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(4) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases specified in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years, respectively.
(5) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be dispensed with – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence has resulted from it.
23

## 3. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another person or other persons by violating the rules of railway, air or water traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
(3) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases specified in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years, respectively.
(4) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be dispensed with – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence has resulted from it.
23

## 4. §

(1) Whoever exposes the life or physical integrity of another person or other persons to direct danger by violating the rules of road traffic on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
23

## 5. §

(1) Whoever causes serious bodily harm to another person or other persons by negligence by violating the rules of road traffic shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes death,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
23

## 6. §

(1) Whoever operates a railway or air vehicle, a mechanically propelled floating facility, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic while intoxicated shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) The punishment shall be for a felony
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
(3) Whoever causes the consequence specified in paragraph (2) by operating a non-mechanically propelled floating facility or a non-mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic while intoxicated shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.
23

## 7. §

(1) Whoever operates a railway or air vehicle, a mechanically propelled floating facility, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic under the influence of a substance having an adverse effect on driving ability other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) The punishment shall be for a felony
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious deterioration of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or a fatal mass disaster.
(3) Whoever causes the consequence specified in paragraph (2) by operating a non-mechanically propelled floating facility or a non-mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic under the influence of a substance having an adverse effect on driving ability other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.
23

## 8. §

(1) Whoever allows a person in an intoxicated or drugged state, or a person otherwise unfit to drive, to drive a railway or air vehicle, a mechanically propelled floating installation, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The penalty for a felony shall be
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offence causes permanent disability, serious injury to health or a mass accident,
b) imprisonment from one to five years if the offence causes death,
c) imprisonment from two to eight years if the offence causes the death of more than two persons or a fatal mass accident.
23

## 9. §

If the driver of a vehicle involved in a traffic accident does not stop at the scene or leaves the scene before ascertaining whether anyone has been injured or requires assistance due to danger directly threatening their life or physical integrity, unless a more serious offence is committed, they shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
239/A. § * (1) Whoever drives on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic a road vehicle defined in the Act on passenger transport services or a vehicle providing special passenger transport services that has been modified without authorisation or in a manner deviating from the authorisation, unless a more serious offence is committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offence is committed with a school bus or a road vehicle intended for the transport of persons with special needs – in particular persons with reduced mobility, elderly or sick persons.
(3) Whoever modifies or has modified without authorisation a vehicle defined in this section shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by confinement.
239/B. § * (1) Whoever drives a vehicle falling within the scope of a driving ban while the driving ban is in effect shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(2) Whoever commits the offence while a driving ban imposed in criminal proceedings is in effect shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
24

## 0. §

(1) The provisions established for offences committed on public roads shall also apply where a violation of the rules on road vehicle driving causes at least serious bodily harm or death not on a public road.
(2) For the purposes of sections 233–23

## 5. §

provisions relating to pedestrians and passengers shall not be considered traffic rules.
(3) * For the purposes of sections 236 and 23

## 8. §

a person in an intoxicated state is a person whose body contains alcohol derived from the consumption of alcoholic beverages capable of producing a blood alcohol concentration exceeding 0.50 grams/litre or a breath alcohol concentration exceeding 0.25 milligrams/litre.
24

## 1. §

(1) Whoever, by significant pollution or otherwise,
a) endangers,
b) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state can only be restored by intervention,
c) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state cannot be restored,
the soil, air, water, wildlife and their components, shall be punishable for a felony in the case specified in point a) by imprisonment not exceeding three years, in the case specified in point b) by imprisonment from one to five years, in the case specified in point c) by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever commits environmental damage by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour in the case of paragraph (1) point a) by imprisonment not exceeding one year, in the case of point b) by imprisonment not exceeding two years, in the case of point c) by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) * In the cases specified in paragraph (1) point a) and in the first and second parts of paragraph (2), the offender shall not be punishable, and in the case of paragraph (1) point b) their punishment may be reduced without limitation, if they eliminate the danger or environmental damage caused by the offence and restore the original state of the damaged environment by the time the first-instance decision on the merits is delivered.
(4) For the purposes of this section, pollution means: the loading of the soil, air, water, wildlife and their components exceeding the emission limit values established in legislation or in an official decision.
24

## 2. § (1) Whoever

a) unlawfully acquires, keeps, places on the market, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it, trades in, damages or destroys a specimen of a strictly protected living organism,
b) specimens of a protected living organism or of a plant or animal species of conservation importance in the European Union, provided that their combined value expressed in money as determined by separate legislation reaches the lowest value expressed in money established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,
c) a specimen of a living organism falling under Annexes A and B of the EC Council Regulation on the protection of wild fauna and flora species by regulating trade in them,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the damage to nature causes the destruction of specimens of living organisms to such an extent
a) that in the case of paragraph (1) points a) or b) the combined value expressed in money as determined by separate legislation of the destroyed specimens of living organisms reaches twice the highest value expressed in money established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,
b) that in the case of paragraph (1) point c) endangers the survival of the population of the living organism.
(2a) * Punishable according to paragraph (2) is whoever commits the damage to nature by using poison or by placing bait capable of destroying animals, endangering the life of several animals.
(3) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(3a) * Whoever commits preparation for the offence defined in paragraph (2) or (2a) shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(4) For the purposes of this section, specimen of a living organism means:
a) all developmental stages, forms and states of the specimen of a living organism,
b) a specimen resulting from the crossing and hybridisation of living organisms,
c) a derivative of a specimen of a living organism, which shall be understood to mean the dead organism and any part thereof or of the specimen of a living organism, as well as any product or preparation made from any of the foregoing or containing a component derived from any of them.
24

## 3. §

(1) Whoever unlawfully significantly alters a Natura 2000 area, a protected cave, a protected natural area or a community of protected living organisms or their habitat shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the damage to nature causes significant damage to or destruction of the Natura 2000 area, the protected cave, the protected natural area or the community of protected living organisms or their habitat.
(3) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(4) For the purposes of this section, Natura 2000 area shall be understood to mean the concept defined in the Act on the protection of nature.
24

## 4. § (1) Whoever

a) unjustifiably mistreats a vertebrate animal in such a manner, or applies such treatment to a vertebrate animal that is capable of causing its permanent injury to health or death,
b) drives away, abandons or exposes his vertebrate animal or dangerous animal,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) * The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the animal cruelty is committed
a) causing particular suffering to the animal,
b) causing permanent injury to health or death of several animals,
c) in front of a large public,
d) against an animal specified in the Act on the protection and care of animals, in respect of which a prohibition from keeping animals for pleasure or keeping the animal species concerned applies, during the period of prohibition, or
e) within two years following conviction for the offence of animal cruelty or organising prohibited animal fights.
(3) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the animal cruelty is committed
a) by the use of poison or by placing bait capable of destroying the animal, causing the death of several animals, or
b) as a special recidivist.
(4) * Whoever commits the offence specified in point a) of paragraph (1) for the purpose of gaining profit in connection with breeding, against more than ten animals kept for pleasure, by neglecting the care of a good manager, shall be punishable according to paragraph (3).
(5) * Whoever commits preparation for the offence specified in point a) of paragraph (3) shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(6) * For the purposes of point b) of paragraph (3), for the purposes of special recidivism, offences of a similar nature are environmental damage causing the death of an animal, animal cruelty, organising prohibited animal fights.
24

## 5. § Whoever

a) carries out activity aimed at killing or capturing game in a hunting area without entitlement to hunt, or in another person's hunting area without permission as a hunter,
b) kills or captures an individual of a game species subject to a hunting prohibition period specified in separate legislation, extending to all individuals of the game species,
c) carries out activity aimed at killing or capturing game or strictly protected or protected vertebrate animal with prohibited hunting equipment specified in separate legislation, by prohibited hunting method or in a sanctuary area,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
24

## 6. § Whoever

a) unlawfully carries out activity aimed at catching fish with a fishing net or other fishing equipment – excluding angling,
b) carries out activity aimed at catching fish with prohibited equipment specified in separate legislation, by prohibited method or in a sanctuary area,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
24

## 7. §

(1) Whoever organises or holds an animal fight with the participation of vertebrate animals, organises betting on such animal fight or places a bet, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(1a) * Whoever participates in an animal fight shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) Whoever acquires, keeps, breeds, trains, tames or trades vertebrate animals for the purpose of animal fighting shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(3) * If the organising of prohibited animal fights is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case specified in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) in the cases specified in paragraphs (1a) and (2), imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(4) * For the purposes of paragraph (3), for the purposes of special recidivism, offences of a similar nature are environmental damage committed causing the death of an animal, animal cruelty, organising prohibited animal fights, breach of obligations in connection with keeping a dangerous animal or dangerous dog.
24

## 8. § * (1) Whoever

a) carries out waste management activity without registration or notification, or without permission or exceeding the limits of the permission, or
b) carries out other unlawful activity with waste and it is capable of endangering human life, physical integrity, health, the earth, water, air or their components, or individuals of living organisms
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) Whoever places waste in a location not authorised by the authority for that purpose
a) capable of endangering human life, physical integrity, health, the earth, water, air or their components, or individuals of living organisms, or
b) in a significant quantity
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the offence specified in paragraph (1) or (2) is committed
a) in respect of hazardous waste,
b) in respect of a particularly significant quantity of waste, or
c) as a special recidivist.
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offence specified in point a) of paragraph (3) also qualifies according to point b) of paragraph (3).
(5) Whoever commits the offence by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment
a) in the cases specified in paragraphs (1) and (2), not exceeding one year,
b) in the case specified in paragraph (3), not exceeding two years,
c) in the case specified in paragraph (4), not exceeding three years.
(6) For the purposes of this §
a) for the purposes of special recidivism, offences of a similar nature are environmental damage and damage to nature;
b) waste management activity means waste management according to the Act on waste, with the provision that the concept of transport must include import into the country, export from it, and transit through it;
c) significant quantity of waste means waste exceeding 1000 kg or 10 cubic metres;
d) particularly significant quantity of waste means ten times the significant quantity of waste.
24

## 9. §

(1) Whoever manufactures, uses, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it or places on the market an ozone-depleting substance or a product containing such substance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) Whoever commits abuse with ozone-depleting substance by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
25

## 0. §

(1) Whoever, in respect of dangerous radioactive material, without permission or notification, or exceeding the limits of the permission
a) produces, stores, places, transports,
b) acquires, possesses, handles, places on the market, processes or otherwise uses, transfers to a person not entitled to keep it, disposes of, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, or transports through it,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence in a criminal organisation shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for abuse with radioactive material shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(4) * Whoever commits the offence specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(5) For the purposes of this §, dangerous radioactive material means: a substance occurring in nature or artificially produced, one or more components of which emit ionising radiation, and which is dangerous to human life and health, or to the living and non-living environment.
25

## 1. §

(1) Whoever operates a nuclear facility without permission or exceeding the limits of the permission shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence specified in paragraph (1) in a criminal organisation shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for abuse with the operation of a nuclear facility shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
25

## 2. §

(1) Whoever deceives the authority or person entitled to make a decision in order to obtain a permit necessary for the application of nuclear energy shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) Whoever fails to fulfil their obligation to notify in connection with the application of nuclear energy shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
25

## 3. §

Against the perpetrator of environmental damage, damage to nature, poaching, illegal fishing, violation of the rules of waste management and the organisation of prohibited animal fights, expulsion may also be imposed.
253/A. § * For the purposes of this chapter, poison means a hazardous substance or mixture which, based on its toxicological properties, in case of inhalation, ingestion or absorption through the skin, endangers the life of the animal or may cause particular suffering or permanent damage to the health of the animal.
25

## 4. §

(1) Whoever commits an act directly aimed at changing the constitutional order of Hungary by force or by threatening with it shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment.
(2) Whoever commits preparation for the violent change of the constitutional order shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(3) The person whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the commission of the offence, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for the violent change of the constitutional order.
25

## 5. §

(1) Whoever establishes or leads an organisation whose purpose is to change the constitutional order of Hungary by force or by threatening with it shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(2) Whoever participates in the organisation defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) The person whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the conspiracy, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for conspiracy against the constitutional order.
25

## 6. §

(1) Whoever participates in a riot whose direct purpose is to
a) the National Assembly,
b) the President of the Republic,
c) * the Curia,
d) * the Government or
e) * the Constitutional Court
prevent by force or by threatening with it from exercising the powers defined in the Fundamental Law, or to coerce into taking action, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever organises or leads the riot defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for insurrection shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) The participant who leaves the riot voluntarily or upon the call of the authority before the commencement of violent acts shall not be punishable for insurrection.
25

## 7. §

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of disrupting the constitutional order of Hungary, destroys, renders unusable or damages a public utility, production, public transport or communication facility or its equipment, public building or structure, product stock, war material or other property of similarly important nature by its purpose, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the sabotage results in particularly serious disadvantage.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for sabotage shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
25

## 8. §

(1) A Hungarian citizen who establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organisation for the purpose of violating the independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order of Hungary shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the treason is committed
a) causing serious disadvantage,
b) by using state service or official mandate,
c) in time of war or
d) by calling in or using foreign armed forces.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for treason shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years, in time of war from two to eight years.
25

## 9. §

A Hungarian citizen who, by abusing their state service or official mandate, establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organisation, and thereby endangers the independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order of Hungary, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years, in time of war from five to fifteen years.
26

## 0. §

(1) Whoever, in time of war, for the purpose of weakening the military strength of Hungary, enters into contact with the enemy, provides assistance to it or causes disadvantage to their own or allied armed forces, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.
(2) Whoever commits preparation for supporting the enemy shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
26

## 1. §

(1) Whoever conducts espionage activity against Hungary for a foreign power or foreign organisation shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever commits the espionage defined in paragraph (1) by delivering strictly confidential classified data shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for espionage shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) The person who – before conducting any other espionage activity – reports their offer or undertaking to the authority or the competent body of the state and fully discloses their foreign contact shall not be punishable for offering or undertaking espionage activity.
261/A. § * The person who conducts espionage activity for a third state outside the European Union against the European Parliament, the European Commission or the Council of the European Union shall be punishable according to § 26

## 1. §

26

## 2. §

The person who commits espionage on the territory of Hungary or a state allied with Hungary according to a valid international treaty containing the obligation of mutual military assistance, against allied armed forces, shall be punishable according to § 26

## 1. §

26

## 3. §

(1) * Whoever obtains credible knowledge that violent change of the constitutional order, conspiracy against the constitutional order, insurrection, sabotage, treason, disloyalty, supporting the enemy, espionage, espionage committed against allied armed forces, espionage against the institutions of the European Union is being prepared, or such an offence not yet uncovered has been committed, and does not report this to the authority or the competent body of the state as soon as possible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) A relative of the perpetrator shall not be punishable for failure to report an offence against the state.
26

## 4. §

In the case of the offences defined in this chapter, expulsion may also be imposed against the perpetrator.
26

## 5. § (1) Whoever, with respect to classified information,

a) unlawfully obtains or uses it,
b) makes it accessible to an unauthorised person, or makes it inaccessible to an authorised person,
commits misuse of classified information.
(2) The punishment is
a) detention for a misdemeanour, if it is of restricted distribution,
b) imprisonment not exceeding one year, if it is confidential,
c) imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony, if it is secret,
d) imprisonment from one to five years, if it is top secret
classified information with respect to which the offence is committed.
(3) A person authorised by law to use classified information who commits misuse of classified information with respect to information classified as restricted distribution, confidential, secret or top secret shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year, two years, from one to five years, or from two to eight years, respectively, according to the distinction set out in paragraph (2).
(4) Whoever commits preparation for misuse of classified information as defined in paragraph (2) points c)–d) shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour, or not exceeding three years for a felony, respectively, according to the distinction made therein.
(5) * A person authorised by law to use classified information who commits preparation for misuse of classified information as defined in paragraph (2) points c)–d) shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years, or from one to five years, respectively, for a felony according to the distinction made therein.
(6) A person authorised by law to use classified information who commits the offence by negligence shall be punishable by detention for a misdemeanour, or by imprisonment not exceeding one year, two years, or three years, respectively, according to the distinction set out in paragraph (2).
26

## 6. §

(1) Criminal law protection extends, for thirty days from the initiation of classification, also to information for which classification has been initiated but the classification procedure has not yet been completed at the time of commission of the offence, and the offender is aware of this.
(2) *
26

## 7. § * (1) Whoever

a) makes data managed in a state register forming part of the national data assets inaccessible to the data controller, or
b) unlawfully or in breach of the limits of their authorisation obstructs the operation of a state register forming part of the national data assets,
if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if
a) the offence causes significant harm to interests, or
b) the offence is committed for gain.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if, as a consequence of the offence, all data managed in a state register forming part of the national data assets become permanently inaccessible to the data controller.
26

## 8. § (1) Whoever

a) falsely accuses another person before an authority of committing an offence,
b) brings to the attention of an authority fabricated evidence relating to an offence against another person,
shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if criminal proceedings are instituted against the person concerned on the basis of the false accusation.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if
a) the accused is convicted on the basis of the false accusation,
b) the false accusation relates to an offence for which the law also provides for life imprisonment.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the accused is convicted on the basis of a false accusation relating to an offence punishable by life imprisonment.
(5) Whoever falsely accuses another person before an authority of committing an offence because, by negligence, they are unaware that their statement of fact is false or the evidence is false, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.
26

## 9. § Whoever

a) falsely accuses another person before an authority of a petty offence or an infringement punishable by administrative fine,
b) falsely accuses another person before an authority or a person exercising disciplinary jurisdiction of a disciplinary offence,
c) brings to the attention of an authority or a person exercising disciplinary jurisdiction fabricated evidence relating to a petty offence, an infringement punishable by administrative fine or a disciplinary offence against another person,
shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanour.
27

## 0. §

(1) If proceedings have been instituted as a result of a false accusation, criminal proceedings for false accusation may be instituted only on the basis of a report by the authority conducting the main case until the conclusion of that main case. Except in the case of such a report, the limitation period for the punishability of false accusation begins on the day of the conclusion of the main case.
(2) The punishment of the perpetrator of false accusation may be reduced without limit, and in cases deserving special leniency may be dispensed with, if they disclose the falsity of the accusation to the authority conducting the proceedings before the conclusion of the main case.
27

## 1. §

(1) Whoever makes a report to an authority which they know to be false and which serves as the basis for criminal proceedings – and the case of 26

## 8. §

does not apply –, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.
(2) Whoever makes a report to an authority, or to a person exercising disciplinary jurisdiction, which they know to be false and which serves as the basis for petty offence or disciplinary proceedings – and the case of 26

## 9. § does not apply –, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
