(3) Whoever makes a report to an authority which they know to be false and which serves as the basis for other official proceedings – and the case of 26

## 9. § does not apply –, shall be punishable by detention.

27

## 2. §

(1) A witness who gives false testimony before an authority concerning a material circumstance of the case, or conceals the truth, commits false testimony.
(2) The provisions on false testimony shall apply to a person who
a) as an expert gives a false expert opinion or as an expert adviser gives false information,
b) as an interpreter or translator translates falsely,
c) outside the case of 26

## 8. §

(1) point b), provides a false document or false physical evidence in criminal or civil proceedings.
(3) * The accused in criminal proceedings may not be punished under paragraph (2) point c).
(4) * Whoever commits false testimony in criminal proceedings shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony. If the false testimony relates to an offence also punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(5) Whoever commits false testimony in civil proceedings shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years. If the subject matter of the civil case is property of particularly high value or another interest of particular significance, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.
(6) Whoever commits false testimony by negligence shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanour.
27

## 3. §

Whoever commits false testimony in petty offence or other official proceedings, or in disciplinary proceedings, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanour.
27

## 4. §

Criminal proceedings for false testimony may be instituted only on the basis of a report by the authority conducting the main case in which the false testimony was committed, until such time as that main case is concluded. Except in the case of such a report, the limitation period for the punishability of false testimony begins on the day of the conclusion of the main case.
27

## 5. § (1) The following may not be punished for false testimony:

a) a person who, if the truth were revealed, would accuse themselves or a relative of committing an offence,
b) a person who may refuse to give testimony on other grounds but was not cautioned of this before being heard, or
c) a person whose hearing is excluded by law.
(2) * The punishment may be reduced without limit, and in cases deserving special leniency may be dispensed with, in respect of a person who, before the final or definitive conclusion of the main case, reports to the authority conducting the proceedings the false nature of the evidence provided by them.
27

## 6. §

(1) * Whoever attempts to induce another person to commit perjury is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if committed in criminal proceedings, or by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor if committed in civil proceedings.
(2) Whoever commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) in proceedings pending before a misdemeanor or other authority, or before a person exercising disciplinary jurisdiction, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanor.
27

## 7. §

* A witness who unlawfully refuses to testify before a court in criminal proceedings after being cautioned about the duty to testify is punishable by confinement for a misdemeanor.
27

## 8. §

(1) Whoever compels another person by force or threat not to exercise their lawful rights or not to fulfill their obligations in official proceedings commits the offense of coercion in official proceedings.
(2) * Whoever commits the offense in criminal proceedings is punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony. If the offense is committed in criminal proceedings concerning an offense punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) Whoever commits the offense in civil proceedings is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years. If the subject matter of the civil case is property of particularly high value or a particularly significant other interest, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) If the offense is committed in proceedings pending before a misdemeanor or other authority, or before a person exercising disciplinary jurisdiction, the punishment is imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanor.
27

## 9. §

(1) * Whoever engages in provocatively anti-social or violent conduct that disrupts, obstructs or frustrates the proceedings of an authority is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor, if a more serious offense is not committed.
(2) * The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in court proceedings.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group, while armed or in an armed state.
28

## 0. §

(1) Whoever discloses a fact, data or circumstance that came to their knowledge as an official witness without being released from this obligation by the person authorized to do so is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanor.
(2) If a more serious offense is not committed, whoever discloses what was said at a closed court hearing without the permission of the person authorized to do so is punishable pursuant to paragraph (1). *
(3) Whoever discloses a fact or data that may not be made public and that was said at a judicial council session or is contained in the minutes of the council session or its annexes is punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).
28

## 1. §

(1) Whoever fails to communicate to a person subject to criminal proceedings, their defense counsel or the authority a fact upon which the termination of proceedings against the person subject to criminal proceedings or the acquittal of the person subject to proceedings may depend is punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.
(2) If the concealment of an exculpatory circumstance relates to an offense punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) Whoever fails to communicate to a person subject to misdemeanor proceedings, administrative proceedings for violations punishable by administrative fine, or disciplinary proceedings, their legal representative or the authority a fact upon which the termination of proceedings against the person subject to proceedings may depend is punishable by confinement for a misdemeanor.
(4) The following are not punishable for concealment of an exculpatory circumstance:
a) a person who by communicating the fact would accuse themselves or their relative
aa) of an offense in criminal proceedings or official proceedings,
ab) of an offense or misdemeanor in misdemeanor proceedings,
ac) of an offense, misdemeanor, violation punishable by administrative fine, or disciplinary offense in disciplinary proceedings,
b) a person whose examination as a witness is excluded by law.
28

## 2. §

(1) Whoever, without having agreed with the perpetrator of the offense before its commission,
a) provides assistance so that the perpetrator may escape from pursuit by the authorities,
b) attempts to frustrate the success of criminal proceedings, or
c) participates in securing the benefit derived from the offense,
is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if harboring a criminal is committed for profit.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if harboring a criminal is committed in connection with
a) an offense defined in Chapter XIII, XIV or XXIV [except failure to report an offense against the state [26

## 3. § paragraph (1)],

b) * homicide [16

## 0. §

paragraphs (1)–(3) and (5)], kidnapping [19

## 0. §

paragraphs (1)–(4)], human trafficking and forced labor [19

## 2. §

paragraphs (1)–(6)], act of terrorism [31

## 4. §

paragraphs (1)–(2)], financing of terrorism [31

## 8. §

and 318/A. §], hijacking of a vehicle [32

## 0. § paragraphs (1)–(2)],

c) an offense punishable by life imprisonment not listed in points a)–b), or
d) is committed by an official or foreign official in the course of official proceedings in breach of official duty, or by a person performing a public function in the course of their proceedings.
(4) Except for paragraph (2) and paragraph (3) point d), a person who commits harboring a criminal as defined in paragraph (1) point a) in the interest of their relative is not punishable.
28

## 3. §

(1) Whoever escapes from the custody of the authorities during criminal proceedings or during the execution of imprisonment or confinement is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) * Whoever, for the purpose of evading criminal proceedings conducted against them, leaves during criminal supervision the area, dwelling, other premises, institution, or enclosed area belonging thereto designated for them in breach of the rules of the coercive measure is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.
(2a) * Whoever, for the purpose of evading extradition or surrender proceedings conducted against them, leaves during extradition supervision, provisional extradition supervision, provisional surrender supervision, or provisional enforcement criminal supervision the area, dwelling, other premises, institution, or enclosed area belonging thereto designated for them in breach of the rules of the coercive measure is punishable pursuant to paragraph (2).
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding one year if the convicted person during the execution of imprisonment does not return after the expiry of the interruption of sentence, leave, short-term leave or temporary release granted to them for the purpose of evading the execution of the sentence.
(3a) * The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding one year if the convicted person during reintegration custody leaves the dwelling designated for them and the enclosed area belonging thereto or the place of stay according to the daily schedule designated for them in breach of the rules of reintegration custody for the purpose of evading the execution of imprisonment.
(3b) * If a person subject to proceedings escapes from the custody of the authorities during misdemeanor detention or the execution of misdemeanor confinement, they are punishable by confinement for a misdemeanor.
(4) The punishment of the perpetrator of escape from custody may be reduced without limitation if they voluntarily surrender before their whereabouts become known to the authorities.
28

## 4. §

(1) A detainee who participates in open defiance that seriously endangers the order of detention together with other detainees shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The prison riot shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for
a) the initiator, organizer or leader,
b) a participant who uses violence against a person acting against the prison riot.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the prison riot leads to particularly serious consequences.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the prison riot causes death.
(5) A person who commits preparatory acts for a prison riot shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.
(6) In the case defined in paragraph (1), the punishment of a person who voluntarily or upon the request of the authority ceases the defiance may be mitigated without limitation.
28

## 5. §

(1) An attorney who, in order to cause unlawful disadvantage to his client, violates his professional duty shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the abuse of attorney's position is committed for the purpose of gaining profit.
(3) For the purposes of this §, an attorney also includes a trainee attorney and any other person who is entitled to legal representation by virtue of his occupation.
28

## 6. §

(1) * A person who unlawfully and professionally performs attorney, patent agent or notarial activities shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.
(2) * The punishment is imprisonment up to three years for a felony if the unauthorized practice is committed by simulating entitlement to attorney, patent agent or notarial activities.
28

## 7. § (1) A person who

a) removes or violates the seal applied during seizure, attachment or sequestration ordered in official proceedings,
b) opens a closed room serving for the safekeeping of a seized, attached or sequestered thing,
c) * makes data subject to an obligation to preserve electronic data accessible to an unauthorized person, or withdraws or modifies it from the proceedings,
d) * makes electronic data temporarily made inaccessible during criminal proceedings accessible to an unauthorized person, or withdraws or modifies it from the proceedings,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
(2) A person who withdraws a thing seized, attached or sequestered during official proceedings from enforcement shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(3) * The perpetrator shall not be punishable for breaking seals as defined in paragraph (2) if, before the indictment, he returns the thing – without damage to its substance – to the proceeding authority.
28

## 8. §

(1) A person who, during judicial enforcement, after the court has imposed a fine on him with final effect, continues the conduct that gave rise to the imposition of the fine, or still does not fulfill his obligation arising from enforcement as set forth in law – not including the obligation set forth in the enforceable instrument – shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
(2) * The perpetrator shall not be punishable if, before the indictment, he fulfills his obligation arising from enforcement as set forth in law.
28

## 9. §

A person who commits the criminal offense defined therein during or in connection with proceedings before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union, shall be punishable according to §§ 268–28

## 2. §

29

## 0. §

(1) A person who gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a person performing activities for or in the interest of a business organization, or to another person in consideration of such person, in order that he violate his duty, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in connection with a person authorized to take independent measures who performs activities for or in the interest of a business organization.
(3) The punishment is
a) in the case of paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,
b) in the case of paragraph (2), imprisonment from two to eight years
if the bribery is committed in a criminal association or professionally.
(4) A person who commits bribery in connection with a person performing activities for or in the interest of a foreign business organization shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(3).
(5) * The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be waived – against the perpetrator of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) if he reports the criminal offense to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and discloses the circumstances of the commission. This provision shall not apply if the criminal offense is committed in a criminal organization.
(6) * A person who, in connection with the provision of health services, gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a health worker, a person working in health care or to another person in consideration of these, as defined in the Act on Health Care, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
29

## 1. §

(1) * A person who, in connection with activities performed for or in the interest of a business organization, requests an unlawful advantage, or accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of an unlawful advantage given or promised to or requested by a third person in consideration of him, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) If the perpetrator
a) violates his duty for the unlawful advantage, he shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years,
b) commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) in a criminal association or professionally, he shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) If the perpetrator is a person authorized to take independent measures who performs activities for or in the interest of a business organization, the punishment is
a) in the case defined in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,
b) in the case defined in paragraph (2) point a), imprisonment from two to eight years,
c) in the case defined in paragraph (2) point b), imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) A person performing activities for or in the interest of a foreign business organization who commits the criminal offense defined therein shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(3).
(5) * The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be waived – against the perpetrator of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) and paragraph (3) point a) if he reports the criminal offense to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority, hands over the received unlawful pecuniary advantage or its equivalent value to the authority, and discloses the circumstances of the commission. This provision shall not apply if the criminal offense is committed in a criminal organization.
(6) * For the purposes of this §, an unlawful advantage in connection with health services means an unlawful advantage as defined in the Act on Health Care.
29

## 2. § *

29

## 3. §

(1) * Whoever seeks to influence an official person in connection with their function by means of an unlawful advantage given or promised to them or to another person in consideration of them, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) * The briber shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years if they give or promise the unlawful advantage in order that the official person violate their official duty, exceed their powers, or otherwise abuse their official position.
(3) * Whoever commits the offence defined in (1) and (2) in connection with the function of a foreign official person shall be punishable according to (1) and (2).
(4) The manager of an economic organisation, or a person authorised to control or supervise and performing activities for or in the interest of the economic organisation, shall be punishable according to (1) if a person performing activities for or in the interest of the economic organisation commits the offence defined in (1)–(3) in the interest of the economic organisation, and the fulfilment of their supervisory or control obligation could have prevented the commission of the offence.
(5) The manager of an economic organisation, or a person authorised to control or supervise and performing activities for or in the interest of the economic organisation, who commits the offence defined in (4) by negligence, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(6) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be dispensed with – in respect of the perpetrator of the offence defined in (1) and (2), if they report the offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.
29

## 4. §

(1) * An official person who, in connection with their function, requests an unlawful advantage, accepts the unlawful advantage or the promise thereof, or agrees with the person requesting or accepting an unlawful advantage requested by or given or promised to a third person in consideration of them, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offence is committed by an official person in a leading position.
(3) In the case defined in (1), the punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years, and in the case defined in (2), imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the person
a) * for the unlawful advantage
aa) violates their official duty,
ab) exceeds their powers, or
ac) otherwise abuses their official position, or
b) commits the offence in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.
(4) A foreign official person who commits the offence defined in (1)–(3) shall be punishable according to (1)–(3).
(5) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be dispensed with – in respect of the perpetrator of the offence defined in (1) and (2), if they report the offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority, hand over the unlawful proprietary advantage received or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of the commission. This provision shall not be applicable if the offence is committed in a criminal organisation.
29

## 5. §

(1) * Whoever gives or promises an unlawful advantage to another person or to a person in consideration of them in order that the other person not exercise their lawful rights or not fulfil their obligations in judicial, arbitration or authority proceedings, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence defined in (1) in the course of or in connection with proceedings before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union, shall be punishable according to (1).
(3) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be dispensed with – in respect of the perpetrator of the offence defined in (1) and (2), if they report the offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.
29

## 6. §

(1) * Whoever requests an unlawful advantage, accepts the unlawful advantage or the promise thereof, or agrees with the person requesting or accepting an unlawful advantage requested by or given or promised to a third person in consideration of them, in order not to exercise their lawful rights or not to fulfil their obligations in judicial, arbitration or authority proceedings, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence defined in (1) in the course of or in connection with proceedings before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union, shall be punishable according to (1).
(3) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be dispensed with – in respect of the perpetrator of the offence defined in (1) and (2), if they report the offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority, hand over the unlawful proprietary advantage received or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of the commission.
29

## 7. § *

29

## 8. § (1) Whoever

a) gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a person who claims to influence an official person, or
b) * gives or promises an unlawful advantage in consideration of a person who claims to influence an official person, to another person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(1a) * Whoever gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a person presenting themselves as an official person or to another person in consideration of them shall be punishable according to (1).
(2) * Whoever commits the offence defined in (1) in connection with a person performing activities for or in the interest of an economic organisation shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(3) * Whoever commits the offence defined in (1) and (1a) in connection with a foreign official person shall be punishable according to (1).
(4) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be dispensed with – in respect of the perpetrator of the offence defined in (1)–(2), if they report the offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.
29

## 9. §

* (1) * Whoever, on the grounds that they influence an official person, requests an unlawful advantage for themselves or another, accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of an unlawful advantage requested by or given or promised to a third party in respect of them, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the perpetrator
a) claims or creates the appearance that they are bribing an official person,
b) passes themselves off as an official person, or
c) commits the criminal offence as a business.
(2a) * Punishable according to paragraph (2) is whoever, passing themselves off as an official person, requests an unlawful advantage in connection with this simulated activity, or accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise.
(3) Whoever commits the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) in connection with a person performing activities for or in the interest of an economic organisation is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to two years.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment up to three years for a felony if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (3) is committed in connection with a person performing activities for or in the interest of an economic organisation who is authorised to take independent measures.
(5) Punishable according to paragraphs (1) and (2) is whoever commits the criminal offence defined therein in connection with a foreign official person.
(6) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be dispensed with – against the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (3) if, before the criminal offence came to the knowledge of the authority, they report it to the authority, hand over the unlawful pecuniary advantage received or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of the commission.
30

## 0. §

* (1) An official person who, in this capacity, obtains credible knowledge that as yet undetected bribery, acceptance of bribery, official bribery, acceptance of official bribery, bribery in judicial or official proceedings, acceptance of bribery in judicial or official proceedings, trading in influence or influence peddling has been committed, and does not report this to the authority as soon as they can, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) A relative of the perpetrator is not punishable for failure to report a corruption offence.
300/A. § * (1) For the purposes of this chapter, breach of duty also includes the performance of a duty tied to the giving of an advantage.
(2) For the purposes of § 29

## 0. §

and § 29

## 1. §

a foreign economic organisation is an organisation which has legal personality under its personal law and is entitled to carry out economic activity in the given organisational form.
30

## 1. §

(1) An official person who physically assaults another during their proceedings is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.
(3) Whoever commits a preparatory act directed at the criminal offence of assault in official proceedings is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to one year.
(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of the commission of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the bringing of charges may be mitigated without limitation.
30

## 2. §

(1) A person performing a public duty who physically assaults another during the performance of their public duty is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.
(3) Whoever commits a preparatory act directed at the criminal offence of assault in proceedings by a person performing a public duty is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to one year.
(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of the commission of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the bringing of charges may be mitigated without limitation.
30

## 3. §

(1) An official person who, in order that another make or not make a statement or declaration, applies violence, threat or other similar method is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.
(3) Whoever commits a preparatory act directed at coerced interrogation is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to two years.
(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of the commission of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the bringing of charges may be mitigated without limitation.
30

## 4. §

(1) An official person who, during their proceedings, unlawfully deprives another of their personal liberty is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the unlawful detention is committed
a) from a base motive or for a base purpose,
b) with the maltreatment of the victim, or
c) causing a serious consequence.
30

## 5. §

An official person who, in order to cause unlawful detriment or obtain an unlawful advantage,
a) breaches their official duty,
b) exceeds their official authority, or
c) otherwise abuses their official position,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
30

## 6. §

A person performing a public duty who, in order to cause unlawful detriment or obtain an unlawful advantage,
a) breaches their duty related to the public duty,
b) exceeds their authority related to the public duty, or
c) otherwise abuses their public duty position,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
30

## 7. § (1) * An official person who

a) conducts covert information gathering subject to the authorisation of a judge or the minister responsible for justice without authorisation, or applies a covert device subject to judicial authorisation without authorisation, or exceeds the limits of the authorisation for these,
b) unlawfully orders or authorises covert information gathering subject to the authorisation of a judge or the minister responsible for justice, or the application of a covert device subject to judicial authorisation,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) * Punishable according to paragraph (1) is an official person on the basis of whose false statement of fact covert information gathering subject to the authorisation of a judge or the minister responsible for justice, or the application of a covert device subject to judicial authorisation, is ordered or authorised.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) causes significant harm to interests.
30

## 8. § (1) An official person who conducts a reliability screening

a) * without prosecutorial approval, or exceeds the limits of the approval,
b) unlawfully approves,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1) is an official person who states a false fact in the decision ordering the reliability screening, and on this basis the person entitled to do so approves the ordering of the reliability screening.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) causes significant harm to interests.
30

## 9. §

The provisions of this chapter shall also apply accordingly to a member of an authority of a foreign state performing judicial or law enforcement duties who acts on the territory of Hungary on the basis of law.
31

## 0. § (1) Whoever obstructs, compels or assaults an official or foreign official person

a) obstructs by force or threat in their lawful proceedings,
b) compels by force or threat to take action in their lawful proceedings, or
c) assaults during or because of their proceedings,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if violence against an official person is committed in a group, armed or in an armed manner.
(3) The organizer or leader of the group defined in paragraph (2) shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) Whoever participates in a group aimed at committing violence against an official person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years, and the organizer and leader of the group shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) Whoever assaults an official person or foreign official person because of their proceedings shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1)-(4) even if the assaulted person is no longer an official person or foreign official person at the time of committing the offense.
(6) Whoever commits preparation for violence against an official person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
(7) The participant of the group cannot be punished under paragraph (4) if they leave the group voluntarily or upon the authority's request.
31

## 1. §

Whoever commits the offense defined therein against a person performing public duties shall be punishable according to 31

## 0. §

31

## 2. §

Whoever commits the offense defined therein against a person who comes to the support or defense of an official person, foreign official person or person performing public duties shall be punishable according to 31

## 0. §

31

## 3. § (1) Whoever

a) assaults,
b) deprives of personal liberty, or
c) endangers
an internationally protected person staying in the country shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) Whoever threatens to commit the offense defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for violence against an internationally protected person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
(4) *
31

## 4. § (1) Whoever, for the purpose of

a) coercing a state body, another state or international organization to do, not do or tolerate something,
b) intimidating the population,
c) changing or disrupting the constitutional, social or economic order of another state, or disrupting the operation of an international organization,
commits a violent, public danger-causing or weapon-related offense against the person defined in paragraph (4), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment.
(2) * Whoever
a) obtains control of significant material assets for the purpose defined in paragraph (1) point a) and makes leaving them intact or returning them dependent on the fulfillment of a demand addressed to a state body or international organization, or
b) * organizes or directs a terrorist group
shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).
(3) The punishment may be reduced without limitation for whoever
a) abandons the terrorist act defined in paragraph (1) or (2) before serious consequences arise from it, and
b) discloses their activity to the authority,
if thereby they cooperate in preventing or mitigating the consequences of the offense, detecting further perpetrators, or preventing further offenses.
(4) For the purposes of this §, violent, public danger-causing or weapon-related offenses against a person are
a) homicide [16

## 3. § paragraphs (1)-(4)]

31

## 5. §

(1) * Whoever incites, offers, undertakes to commit the felony defined in 31

## 4. §

paragraph (1) or (2), agrees on joint commission, or provides the necessary conditions or facilitates it for the purpose of aiding the commission, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) * Whoever carries out the acts defined in paragraph (1) for the purpose of committing the felony defined in 31

## 4. §

paragraph (1) or (2) in a terrorist group shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(3) Whoever reports the offense defined in paragraph (1) or (2) to the authority before it comes to the authority's knowledge and discloses the circumstances of the commission cannot be punished.
31

## 6. §

* Whoever threatens to commit a terrorist act shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
316/A. § * (1) Whoever
a) enters, exits or transits through the territory of Hungary for the purpose of committing the felony defined in 31

## 4. §

paragraph (1) or (2) or inciting, offering, undertaking to commit it, agreeing on joint commission, or providing the necessary conditions or facilitating it for the purpose of aiding the commission, or
b) joining a terrorist group
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever organizes the travel defined in paragraph (1) or provides or collects material resources for the purpose of supporting such travel shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).
31

## 7. §

Whoever obtains credible knowledge that the commission of a terrorist act is being prepared and does not report it to the authority as soon as possible shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
31

## 8. § * (1) Whoever

a) provides or collects material resources for ensuring the conditions for a terrorist act,
b) supports with material resources a person preparing to commit a terrorist act, or the perpetrator of a terrorist act, or another person in consideration of such persons, or
c) provides or collects material resources for the purpose of supporting the persons defined in point b),
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (1) for the commission of a terrorist act in a terrorist group or in the interest of a member of a terrorist group, or otherwise supports the activity of a terrorist group, or provides or collects material resources for the purpose of such support of a terrorist group, shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
318/A. § * (1) Whoever
a) provides or collects material resources for ensuring the conditions for a terrorist-type offence,
b) supports with material resources a person preparing to commit a terrorist-type offence, or the perpetrator of a terrorist-type offence, or another person in consideration of such persons, or
c) provides or collects material resources for the purpose of supporting the persons defined in point b),
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) For the purposes of paragraph (1), terrorist-type offence means:
a) homicide [Section 16

## 0.

paragraph (1), Section 16

## 0.

paragraph (2), if the act is directed against a person present at an airport serving international civil aviation or on board an aircraft in flight or on a ship at sea, or against an internationally protected person],
b) bodily harm (Section 16

## 4.

, if the act is directed against a person present at an airport serving international civil aviation or on board an aircraft in flight or on a ship at sea, or against an internationally protected person),
c) kidnapping [Section 19

## 0. paragraphs (1)–(4)],

d) offence against the safety of transport (Section 23

## 2., if the act is directed against an aircraft or a ship at sea)

e) illicit trafficking in radioactive material (Section 25

## 0.),

f) sabotage (Section 25

## 7.),

g) violence against an internationally protected person (Section 31

## 3.),

h) seizure of control of a vehicle (Section 32

## 0.),

i) causing public danger [Section 32

## 2. paragraphs (1)–(3)],

j) disruption of the operation of a utility of public interest (Section 32

## 3.),

k) illicit trafficking in explosives or explosive devices (Section 32

## 4.

, if the act is committed on the premises of a utility of public interest, or a public building or structure),
l) illicit trafficking in firearms or ammunition (Section 32

## 5.

, if the act is committed on the premises of a utility of public interest, or a public building or structure).
318/B. § *
31

## 9.

* (1) For the purposes of Sections 314–31

## 5.

, Section 316/A. and Section 31

## 8.

, terrorist group means a group of three or more persons, organised for a longer period, operating in a coordinated manner, the purpose of which is to commit a terrorist act.
(2) For the purposes of Section 316/A., Section 31

## 8.

and Section 318/A., material resources means the resources, legal documents and instruments defined in Article 1(1) of Council Regulation (EC) No 2580/2001 of 27 December 2001 on specific restrictive measures directed against certain persons and entities with a view to combating terrorism.
32

## 0.

(1) Whoever seizes control of an aircraft, a vehicle for mass transport or mass freight transport by violence, threat, or by rendering another person unconscious or incapable of defence, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the offence causes death.
(3) Whoever commits preparatory acts for the seizure of control of a vehicle shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) The punishment may be reduced without limitation for a person who abandons the offence before serious consequences have resulted from it.
32

## 1.

(1) Whoever invites, offers, undertakes to commit an offence in a criminal organisation, agrees on joint commission, or provides the necessary or facilitating conditions for the purpose of facilitating the commission, or otherwise supports the activity of the criminal organisation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) A person who reports the offence to the authorities and discloses the circumstances of the commission before the offence has come to the knowledge of the authorities shall not be punishable for participation in a criminal organisation.
32

## 2.

(1) Whoever causes public danger by triggering the destructive effect of matter or energy, or obstructs the prevention of public danger or the mitigation of its consequences, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offence is committed
a) in a group,
b) causing particularly large or greater damage, or
c) in complicity.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the offence causes death.
(4) Whoever commits preparatory acts for causing public danger shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) Whoever commits the causing of public danger by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to three years, in case of particularly large or greater damage from one to five years, in case of causing death from two to eight years.
(6) The punishment may be reduced without limitation for a person who voluntarily eliminates the public danger before harmful consequences have resulted from it.
322/A. § * (1) Whoever obstructs the implementation of
a) epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered for the purpose of preventing the introduction or spread of an infectious disease subject to quarantine obligation,
b) epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered during an epidemic,
c) plant health or animal health measures ordered for the purpose of preventing the introduction and removal, and spread, or eliminating the occurrence of infectious animal diseases or plant quarantine pests
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offence is committed in a group.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the offence causes death.
(4) Whoever commits preparatory acts for obstructing epidemiological measures shall be punishable by imprisonment up to one year.
32

## 3.

(1) Whoever disrupts the operation of a utility of public interest to a significant extent shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the offence is committed
a) in a group,
b) in complicity, or
c) causing particularly large damage.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offence is committed
a) armed,
b) in an armed state, or
c) causing particularly significant damage.
(4) Whoever commits preparatory acts for disrupting the operation of a utility of public interest shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to two years.
(5) Whoever commits the offence by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to three years, in case of causing particularly large or greater damage from one to five years.
32

## 4.

(1) Whoever
a) manufactures, acquires, possesses, places on the market, or transfers to a person not authorised to possess explosives, explosive devices or devices for their use without authorisation,
b) brings into, removes from, or transports through the territory of the country without authorisation or exceeding the limits of the authorisation,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offence is committed as a business or in complicity.
(3) Whoever commits preparatory acts for illicit trafficking in explosives or explosive devices shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.
32

## 5. § (1) Whoever

a) * acquires or possesses an operational firearm without authorization,
b) manufactures or places a firearm on the market without authorization,
c) * brings an operational firearm into the territory of the country, takes it out of the country, or transports it through the country without authorization or exceeding the limits of the authorization,
d) * exceeds the limits of the authorization for the manufacture of, trade in, or acquisition or possession of an operational firearm,
e) transfers a firearm held with authorization to a person without authorization,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Whoever shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1)
a) acquires or possesses ammunition for handguns, hunting firearms or sport firearms exceeding a small quantity, or ammunition for other firearms, without authorization,
b) manufactures or places ammunition on the market without authorization,
c) brings ammunition into the territory of the country, takes it out of the country, or transports it through the country without authorization or exceeding the limits of the authorization,
d) exceeds the limits of the authorization for the manufacture, acquisition, possession or trade of ammunition,
e) transfers ammunition for his handgun, hunting firearm or sport firearm held with authorization exceeding a small quantity, or ammunition for other firearms, to a person without authorization.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the offense is committed on a commercial basis or in a criminal organization.
(4) Whoever brings his handgun, hunting firearm or sport firearm held with authorization, or the ammunition for it, into the territory of the country, takes it out of the country, or transports it through the country without notification, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.
(5) * Whoever commits preparation for the offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(3) shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.
(6) For the purposes of this §, small quantity means ammunition not exceeding ten pieces.
32

## 6. § (1) Whoever

a) develops, manufactures,
b) acquires, possesses, uses, unlawfully renders harmless,
c) transfers to a person not authorized to possess it, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of the country, transports through the country, or places on the market,
a weapon prohibited by international treaty shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) Whoever, without authorization or in deviation from the authorization, constructs or operates a facility suitable for the production of weapons prohibited by international treaty, or converts an existing facility for the purpose of manufacturing such weapons, shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).
(3) Whoever
a) provides technical support for the development, manufacture, assembly, quality testing, operation, maintenance or repair of weapons prohibited by international treaty,
b) deceives the authority or person entitled to make the decision in order to obtain the authorization specified by law for the operation of a facility suitable for the production of weapons prohibited by international treaty,
shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the offense defined in paragraph (3) is committed
a) on a commercial basis,
b) in a criminal organization, or
c) as an official person.
(5) Whoever
a) commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) on a commercial basis, or
b) commits the offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) in a criminal organization or as an official person,
shall be punishable by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment.
(6) Whoever commits preparation for abuse of weapons prohibited by international treaty shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(7) Whoever commits the offense described in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.
32

## 7. §

(1) Whoever violates
a) the obligation to freeze funds or economic resources, or
b) an economic, commercial or financial prohibition
ordered on the basis of Hungary's international legal obligation as promulgated, or in regulations adopted pursuant to Articles 75 and 215 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union or in regulations or decisions adopted on the basis of authorization by these regulations, as well as in Council decisions adopted pursuant to Article 29 of the Treaty establishing the European Union, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the violation of the international economic prohibition is committed
a) in connection with the trade of goods applicable during the execution of the death penalty, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment,
b) with violence, or
c) as an official person.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the violation of the international economic prohibition is committed
a) in connection with the trade of firearms, ammunition, explosives, explosive substances, devices for their use or other products intended for military use,
b) armed, or
c) in a criminal organization.
(4) Whoever commits preparation for the violation of an international economic prohibition shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) * For the purposes of this § – unless the legislation promulgating the obligation or prohibition provides otherwise on the basis of the international legal obligation –
a) funds shall mean the assets defined in Article 1(l) of Council Regulation (EU) No 267/2012 of 23 March 2012 concerning restrictive measures against Iran and repealing Regulation (EU) No 961/2010 (hereinafter in this paragraph: Regulation 267/2012/EU),
b) freezing of funds shall mean what is defined in Article 1(k) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,
c) economic resources shall mean the assets defined in Article 1(h) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,
d) freezing of economic resources shall mean the assets defined in Article 1(j) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,
e) * goods applicable during the death penalty, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment shall mean the goods defined in Annex II of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 January 2019 concerning trade in certain goods which could be used for capital punishment, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment (hereinafter in this chapter: Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council).
32

## 8. §

(1) Whoever obtains credible knowledge that a violation of an international economic prohibition is being prepared, or that such an offense not yet uncovered has been committed, and does not report it to the authority as soon as possible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.
(2) A relative of the perpetrator shall not be punishable for failure to report a violation of an international economic prohibition.
32

## 9. § (1) Whoever

a) manufactures, places on the market military technology products, or provides military technology services without a permit or exceeding the scope of the permit,
b) uses military technology products in a manner different from the permit,
c) produces, acquires, uses, possesses, transfers, places on the market, brings into the territory of the country, takes out from there, or transports through it prohibited military technology products,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever
a) provides technical support in connection with the development, manufacture, trade, maintenance, repair, detection, identification or proliferation of chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, other nuclear explosives, or missile technology devices capable of delivering them,
b) provides technical assistance in connection with other military use, different from that set out in point a), in relation to a country subject to arms export restrictions binding on Hungary on the basis of international commitments.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a criminal organisation or on a commercial basis.
(4) Whoever commits preparation for the abuse of military technology products or services shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.
(5) For the purposes of this Article
a) permit shall also mean the International Import Certificate and the documents replacing them,
b) * military technology product shall also mean the goods specified in Annex III of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council,
c) * prohibited military technology product means the goods specified in Annex II of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council.
33

## 0. § (1) Whoever

a) places dual-use products in foreign trade, including their transfer within the customs territory of the European Union, without a permit or exceeding the scope of the permit, or
b) uses dual-use products in a manner different from the permit,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) * in connection with chemical substances falling within the scope of Annex 1 of Act LXIII of 2020 on the promulgation of the amendment to the Annex of the Convention on the Prohibition of the Development, Production, Stockpiling and Use of Chemical Weapons and on their Destruction, signed in Paris on 13 January 1993, in relation to their transfer to the territory of Hungary or from there to the customs territory of the European Union, or
b) in relation to nuclear dual-use products.
(3) Whoever commits preparation for the abuse of dual-use products shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.
(4) For the purposes of this Article
a) permit shall also mean the International Import Certificate and the documents replacing them,
b) dual-use product means a product as defined in Article 2(1) of Regulation (EC) No 428/2009 setting up a Community regime for the control of exports, transfer, brokering and transit of dual-use items,
c) * the customs territory of the European Union means the territory defined in Article 4 of Regulation (EU) No 952/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 9 October 2013 laying down the Union Customs Code.
33

## 1. §

* (1) Whoever incites to war before a large public, or otherwise conducts war propaganda, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1), if a more serious offence is not committed, is whoever incites to the support of terrorism before a large public, or otherwise conducts propaganda supporting terrorism.
33

## 2. § * Whoever before a large public

a) against the Hungarian nation,
b) against any national, ethnic, racial, religious group or a member thereof, or
c) against certain groups of the population or their members – in particular on the grounds of disability, gender identity, sexual orientation –
incites to violence or hatred, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.
33

## 3. §

* Whoever before a large public denies, questions, presents in an insignificant light, or seeks to justify the fact of genocide or other crimes against humanity committed by National Socialist or Communist regimes, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.
33

## 4. §

Whoever before a large public uses insulting or degrading expressions concerning the anthem, flag, coat of arms or Holy Crown of Hungary, or otherwise desecrates them, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment for up to one year.
33

## 5. §

* Whoever distributes, uses before a large public, or displays publicly a swastika, SS insignia, arrow cross, hammer and sickle, five-pointed red star or symbols depicting them in a manner capable of disturbing public order – in particular in a manner violating the human dignity or funeral rights of the victims of totalitarian regimes –
a) distributes,
b) uses before a large public, or
c) displays publicly,
if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by detention.
33

## 6. §

Whoever before a large public, in a manner capable of disturbing public order, incites to general disobedience against an order of an authority, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.
33

## 7. §

* (1) Whoever at the scene of a public danger, before a large public, in connection with the public danger, asserts or spreads a false fact or distorts a true fact in such a way that is capable of causing confusion or unrest in a larger group of people at the scene of the public danger, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.
(2) Whoever during a special legal order, before a large public, asserts or spreads a false fact or distorts a true fact in such a way that is capable of hindering or frustrating the effectiveness of defence, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
33

## 8. §

(1) Whoever asserts or spreads a false fact capable of disturbing public order, or creates the appearance that an event involving public danger is threatening to occur, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the threat of public danger has seriously disturbed public order.
33

## 9. §

(1) Whoever engages in provocatively anti-social, violent conduct capable of causing scandal or alarm in others, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment for up to two years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment for up to three years for a felony if the hooliganism is committed
a) in a group,
b) seriously disturbing public order,
c) armed,
d) equipped with weapons, or
e) * at a public event or assembly.
34

## 0. §

(1) * Whoever resists with violence or threat the lawful measures taken by the organiser or host of a public event or assembly to maintain order, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment for up to two years.
(2) If a more serious offence is not committed, punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever unlawfully enters or remains in an area of a sports facility separated from spectators or a specific group of spectators at a sports event, or throws into that area an object endangering the holding of the sports event or the physical integrity of others.
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment for up to three years for a felony if the disturbance of order is committed
a) in a group,
b) armed,
c) equipped with weapons, or
d) as a special recidivist,
e) * in violation of the restrictions ensuring the peaceful nature of the assembly under the Act on the Right of Assembly.
(4) For the purposes of special recidivism, an offence of a similar nature is
a) bodily harm [16

## 4. § (3)–(6) and (8)],

b) violence against an official [31

## 0. §

(1)–(5)], violence against a person performing public duties (31

## 1. §)

, violence against a supporter of an official or a person performing public duties (31

## 2. §),

c) disorderly conduct (33

## 9. §),

d) vandalism [37

## 1. § (1)–(6)]

34

## 1. §

Against the perpetrator of disorderly conduct and breach of the peace, exclusion may also be imposed.
34

## 2. § (1) Whoever

a) produces a false public document, or falsifies the content of a public document,
b) uses a false, falsified or genuine public document issued in another person's name,
c) participates in having false data, facts or statements concerning the existence, modification or termination of a right or obligation recorded in a public document,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(1a) * The offence under paragraph (1) point c) shall not be committed in relation to the registration of an address in the register containing the personal and address data of citizens, if it is done with the consent of the landlord or by registering at one's own property.
(2) Whoever commits preparation for forgery of public documents as defined in paragraph (1) points a) or b) shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(3) Whoever commits the forgery of public documents as defined in paragraph (1) point c) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by confinement.
34

## 3. § (1) An official who, abusing his or her official authority,

a) produces a false public document,
b) falsifies the content of a public document, or
c) falsely records a material fact in a public document,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) The provisions of this Section shall also apply accordingly to a member of a foreign state's authority performing judicial or law enforcement duties who acts on the territory of Hungary pursuant to law.
343/A. § * (1) Whoever
a) issues a document that can be confused with a diploma certifying qualifications obtained in higher education or a certificate certifying qualifications obtained in non-formal education or school-based vocational training, or
b) unlawfully confers
ba) a diploma certifying qualifications,
bb) an academic degree,
bc) a title, or
bd) an abbreviation
as defined in or confusable with those defined in the Act on National Higher Education,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (1) in a criminal organisation or as a business shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
34

## 4. § Whoever

a) produces a false security document authorising entry as defined in separate legislation,
b) falsifies the content of a security document authorising entry,
c) uses a false, falsified or genuine security document authorising entry issued in another person's name,
if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
34

## 5. §

Whoever uses a false, falsified or false-content private document to prove the existence, modification or termination of a right or obligation shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
34

## 6. §

* (1) Whoever, with respect to one or more public documents that are not or not exclusively his or her own,
a) unlawfully obtains them from another person without that person's consent,
b) unlawfully destroys or damages them, or
c) unlawfully takes them from another person or unlawfully conceals them from the person entitled,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) If no other offence is committed, whoever transfers or receives a public document for the purpose of unlawful gain shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).
(3) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (1) with respect to a private document in order to obtain an unlawful advantage or cause an unlawful disadvantage shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
34

## 7. § (1) Whoever

a) removes or falsifies a unique identification mark,
b) obtains, uses or possesses an object whose unique identification mark is false, falsified, or whose unique identification mark has been removed,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed as a business or in a criminal organisation.
(3) For the purposes of this Section, unique identification mark means: the marking applied by the manufacturer or the authority on an object, or on a component thereof, for the purpose of unique identification, where the possession or intended use of such object is subject to official authorisation by law.
34

## 8. §

Whoever, for the purpose of unlawful gain, falsifies the value indicated by the odometer of a road motor vehicle by acting on the instrument or influencing the measurement process, or prevents the operation of the instrument, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
34

## 9. § * (1) Whoever

a) acquires ownership of agricultural and forestry land,
b) establishes a usufruct right or right of use over agricultural and forestry land
by entering into a void contract in circumvention of the statutory prohibition or restriction applicable thereto, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.
(2) Whoever
a) acquires ownership of agricultural and forestry land,
b) establishes a usufruct right or right of use over agricultural and forestry land
uses agricultural and forestry land or collects its yields on the basis of an agreement created to conceal a void contract entered into by him or her in circumvention of a statutory prohibition or restriction, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) * An attorney, chamber legal counsel or notary public who participates in the conclusion of a contract under paragraph (1) shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).
(4) The punishment of the perpetrator of the offence under paragraphs (1) and (2) may be reduced without limitation if he or she reports the offence to the authority and discloses the circumstances of its commission before it comes to the attention of the authority.
349/A. § * (1) Whoever enters into an agreement as a result of which the outcome of a competition or match organised in the competition system of a sports federation or otherwise included in the competition calendar of the sports federation is not determined in accordance with the competition rules or the principles of fair play, if no other offence is committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the offence is committed in a criminal organisation or as a business.
35

## 0. §

* (1) Whoever, during an election, referendum or European Citizens' Initiative falling under the scope of the Act on Electoral Procedure or the Act on the Initiation of Referendums, European Citizens' Initiatives and Referendum Procedure,
a) obtains endorsements in violation of the rules of the nomination procedure by violence, threat, deception or material benefit,
b) obtains signatures for the initiation of a referendum or European Citizens' Initiative by violence, threat, deception or material benefit,
c) votes without entitlement,
d) signs without authorisation or enters false data,
e) obstructs a person entitled to participate in the election, referendum, referendum initiative or European Citizens' Initiative, or seeks to influence them by violence, threat, deception or material benefit,
f) violates the secrecy of the election or referendum,
g) falsifies the result of the election, referendum, referendum initiative or European Citizens' Initiative,
h) accepts material benefit for their endorsement during the nomination procedure, or for their signature during the initiation of a referendum or European Citizens' Initiative,
i) makes their vote conditional on the provision of material benefit and accepts material benefit in this regard,
commits a felony and is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) *
350/A. § * A member, responsible person or leading officer of a nominating organisation within the meaning of the Act on Electoral Procedure, as well as a candidate within the meaning of the Act on Electoral Procedure, who uses prohibited foreign support or a pecuniary advantage derived from an agreement concealing the origin of prohibited foreign support for the purpose of circumventing this prohibition, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
35

## 1. §

(1) Whoever participates in the management of an association dissolved by a court, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) Whoever participates in the operation of an association dissolved by a court in a manner capable of disturbing public order is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.
(3) Whoever provides the conditions necessary for or facilitating the management or operation of an association dissolved by a court, or provides material resources for it, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
35

## 2. § Whoever

a) organises activities aimed at maintaining public safety or public order for which they are not authorised by law, or
b) organises activities creating the appearance of maintaining public safety or public order,
is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.
352/A. § * (1) Whoever unlawfully enters the territory of Hungary protected by a facility ensuring the protection of the state border order through the facility is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) as a participant in a riot.
(3) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (1) while armed or equipped with weapons as a participant in a riot is punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offence defined in paragraph (2) or (3) causes death.
352/B. § * (1) Whoever destroys or damages a facility or device ensuring the protection of the state border order, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) as a participant in a riot.
(3) Whoever commits the offence defined in paragraph (1) while armed or equipped with weapons as a participant in a riot is punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years if the offence defined in paragraph (2) or (3) causes death.
352/C. § * Whoever obstructs work related to the construction or maintenance of a facility ensuring the protection of the state border order, if another criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanour.
35

## 3. §

(1) * Whoever provides assistance to another person for crossing the state border in violation of legal provisions is punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.
(2) * The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the people smuggling is committed
a) for pecuniary gain,
b) by providing assistance to multiple persons for crossing the state border, or
c) by destroying or damaging a facility or device ensuring the protection of the state border order.
(3) * The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the people smuggling is committed
a) by mistreating the smuggled person,
b) while armed,
c) while equipped with weapons,
d) as a business, or
e) in a criminal organisation.
(4) * The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if
a) the people smuggling under paragraph (3) point a) is committed in the manner specified in points b)–e)
b) the people smuggling under paragraph (3) point b) is committed in the manner specified in point a) or points c)–e).
(5) * The organiser or director of the offence defined in paragraph (3) or (4) is punishable by imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
(6) * Whoever commits preparation for people smuggling is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
353/A. § * (1) Whoever participates in enabling another person to initiate asylum or other proceedings in Hungary allowing the acquisition of a residence title by making a false statement or concealing a true fact regarding a circumstance materially affecting the official procedure, or to make a false statement or conceal a true fact regarding a circumstance materially affecting the official procedure in such proceedings, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by confinement for a misdemeanour.
(2) The organiser of the offence defined in paragraph (1) is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
(3) The offence defined in paragraph (1) is punishable according to paragraph (2) if committed
a) for pecuniary gain, or
b) by providing assistance to multiple persons.
(4) The offence is punishable according to paragraph (2) if material resources are provided for the commission of the offence defined in paragraph (1) or (2).
(5) For the offences defined in paragraphs (1)–(4), criminal proceedings may be initiated only on the basis of a complaint by the authority conducting the underlying case in which the offences were committed, as long as that underlying case has not been concluded. Except in the case of such a complaint, the limitation period for the offences defined in paragraphs (1)–(4) begins on the date of conclusion of the underlying case.
(6) The perpetrator of the offence defined in paragraph (1) is not punishable if
a) in the event of disclosure of the truth, the subject of the official procedure would accuse themselves or their relative of committing a criminal offence, or
b) they report the offence to the authority and disclose the circumstances of its commission before it comes to the attention of the authority.
(7) The punishment may be reduced without limitation − in cases deserving special consideration it may be waived − for the perpetrator of the offence defined in paragraphs (2)–(4) if they report the offence to the authority and disclose the circumstances of its commission before it comes to the attention of the authority.
35

## 4. § (1) Whoever

a) in the territory of a Member State of the European Union,
b) in the territory of another state party to the Agreement on the European Economic Area, or
c) in the territory of a state whose nationals have the same legal status as nationals of another state party to the Agreement on the European Economic Area
provides assistance for the purpose of obtaining pecuniary gain to a person who is not a national of any of these states for illegal residence, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) If a more serious criminal offence is not committed, whoever provides assistance for the purpose of obtaining pecuniary gain for the illegal residence in Hungary of a foreign person shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).
35

## 5. §

A person who has reached the age of eighteen and who, for the purpose of obtaining material gain, establishes a family relationship solely for the purpose of obtaining a document certifying the right of residence, or consents to a declaration of full acknowledgement establishing paternity, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
35

## 6. § (1) Whoever employs *

a) a third-country national regularly or permanently, or
b) a significant number of third-country nationals simultaneously
who do not hold a permit issued for the purpose of employment, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) Whoever
a) * employs a third-country national who does not hold a permit issued for the purpose of employment under particularly exploitative working conditions,
b) *
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(3) For the purposes of this §
